
Multiplatform openssl initiation via lib call for multi-key, freq scaling efficiency
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Calibration to work out numbers for 5 mins on different freqs on 4 configurable platforms on the non-RSA main run.
**No MAC as we can't control the clocks at all.
Smoke test to validate each type is working for freq changes. Maybe 20-30 mins.  
Full Run is 12x keygens ~5 mins each, x3 independent randomised runs. About half a day per platform.
Pi5 and RISCV have 4/5 freqs to check, whereas the PC is checked on 200Mhz intervals, overapping in places.
As the PC has so many variants, the runs are 1min each for the PC, which is likely statistically valid on the fast keys. 


== Summary of results -- in-process library call (E2c) ==
Energy per key generated (J per 1000 keys), paired LIVE minus NULL; key generation called directly in the OpenSSL library from one running process.


== Template ==
-- Smoke --
   Smoke Commands and Screen Outputs
   Smoke File and Outputs
-- Full Run --
   Full Commands and Screen Outputs
   Full File and Outputs
